calibrateRelIpCost();
[Warning: Kernel evaluation truncates the Gaussian kernel at 6 sigma by default, which runs much faster
than a wider cutoff; the speed-up grows with tuple size r and multiset size, where a wider cutoff has
many kernel centres and becomes expensive. Relative error is of order exp(-k^2/2): ~1.5e-8 at 6 sigma
(the default), ~3.7e-6 at 5, and ~3.4e-4 at 4. Set mptDefaults('truncationSigmas', Inf) for the
accuracy floor (~7.43 sigma, ~1e-12 error). This warning shows only once per session.]
